    [0m[94mdef[39;49;00m[90m [39;49;00m[92mresolve_name[39;49;00m(name):[90m[39;49;00m
    [90m    [39;49;00m[33m"""[39;49;00m
    [33m    Resolve a name to an object.[39;49;00m
    [33m[39;49;00m
    [33m    It is expected that `name` will be a string in one of the following[39;49;00m
    [33m    formats, where W is shorthand for a valid Python identifier and dot stands[39;49;00m
    [33m    for a literal period in these pseudo-regexes:[39;49;00m
    [33m[39;49;00m
    [33m    W(.W)*[39;49;00m
    [33m    W(.W)*:(W(.W)*)?[39;49;00m
    [33m[39;49;00m
    [33m    The first form is intended for backward compatibility only. It assumes that[39;49;00m
    [33m    some part of the dotted name is a package, and the rest is an object[39;49;00m
    [33m    somewhere within that package, possibly nested inside other objects.[39;49;00m
    [33m    Because the place where the package stops and the object hierarchy starts[39;49;00m
    [33m    can't be inferred by inspection, repeated attempts to import must be done[39;49;00m
    [33m    with this form.[39;49;00m
    [33m[39;49;00m
    [33m    In the second form, the caller makes the division point clear through the[39;49;00m
    [33m    provision of a single colon: the dotted name to the left of the colon is a[39;49;00m
    [33m    package to be imported, and the dotted name to the right is the object[39;49;00m
    [33m    hierarchy within that package. Only one import is needed in this form. If[39;49;00m
    [33m    it ends with the colon, then a module object is returned.[39;49;00m
    [33m[39;49;00m
    [33m    The function will return an object (which might be a module), or raise one[39;49;00m
    [33m    of the following exceptions:[39;49;00m
    [33m[39;49;00m
    [33m    ValueError - if `name` isn't in a recognised format[39;49;00m
    [33m    ImportError - if an import failed when it shouldn't have[39;49;00m
    [33m    AttributeError - if a failure occurred when traversing the object hierarchy[39;49;00m
    [33m                     within the imported package to get to the desired object.[39;49;00m
    [33m    """[39;49;00m[90m[39;49;00m
        [94mglobal[39;49;00m _NAME_PATTERN[90m[39;49;00m
        [94mif[39;49;00m _NAME_PATTERN [95mis[39;49;00m [94mNone[39;49;00m:[90m[39;49;00m
            [90m# Lazy import to speedup Python startup time[39;49;00m[90m[39;49;00m
            [94mimport[39;49;00m[90m [39;49;00m[04m[96mre[39;49;00m[90m[39;49;00m
            dotted_words = [33mr[39;49;00m[33m'[39;49;00m[33m(?![39;49;00m[33m\[39;49;00m[33md)([39;49;00m[33m\[39;49;00m[33mw+)([39;49;00m[33m\[39;49;00m[33m.(?![39;49;00m[33m\[39;49;00m[33md)([39;49;00m[33m\[39;49;00m[33mw+))*[39;49;00m[33m'[39;49;00m[90m[39;49;00m
            _NAME_PATTERN = re.compile([33mf[39;49;00m[33m'[39;49;00m[33m^(?P<pkg>[39;49;00m[33m{[39;49;00mdotted_words[33m}[39;49;00m[33m)[39;49;00m[33m'[39;49;00m[90m[39;49;00m
                                       [33mf[39;49;00m[33m'[39;49;00m[33m(?P<cln>:(?P<obj>[39;49;00m[33m{[39;49;00mdotted_words[33m}[39;49;00m[33m)?)?$[39;49;00m[33m'[39;49;00m,[90m[39;49;00m
                                       re.UNICODE)[90m[39;49;00m
    [90m[39;49;00m
        m = _NAME_PATTERN.match(name)[90m[39;49;00m
        [94mif[39;49;00m [95mnot[39;49;00m m:[90m[39;49;00m
            [94mraise[39;49;00m [96mValueError[39;49;00m([33mf[39;49;00m[33m'[39;49;00m[33minvalid format: [39;49;00m[33m{[39;49;00mname[33m!r}[39;49;00m[33m'[39;49;00m)[90m[39;49;00m
        gd = m.groupdict()[90m[39;49;00m
        [94mif[39;49;00m gd.get([33m'[39;49;00m[33mcln[39;49;00m[33m'[39;49;00m):[90m[39;49;00m
            [90m# there is a colon - a one-step import is all that's needed[39;49;00m[90m[39;49;00m
            mod = importlib.import_module(gd[[33m'[39;49;00m[33mpkg[39;49;00m[33m'[39;49;00m])[90m[39;49;00m
            parts = gd.get([33m'[39;49;00m[33mobj[39;49;00m[33m'[39;49;00m)[90m[39;49;00m
            parts = parts.split([33m'[39;49;00m[33m.[39;49;00m[33m'[39;49;00m) [94mif[39;49;00m parts [94melse[39;49;00m [][90m[39;49;00m
        [94melse[39;49;00m:[90m[39;49;00m
            [90m# no colon - have to iterate to find the package boundary[39;49;00m[90m[39;49;00m
            parts = name.split([33m'[39;49;00m[33m.[39;49;00m[33m'[39;49;00m)[90m[39;49;00m
            modname = parts.pop([94m0[39;49;00m)[90m[39;49;00m
            [90m# first part *must* be a module/package.[39;49;00m[90m[39;49;00m
            mod = importlib.import_module(modname)[90m[39;49;00m
